# Review pack: avot perek-6-02 (Pirkei Avot 6:7–6:11)

Brief: `briefs/review.md`. Write: `works/avot/stage/review/perek-6-02.json`. Check: `go run ./cmd/tr check avot perek-6-02`.

## Pirkei Avot 6:7

Hebrew (Torat Emet): גְּדוֹלָה תוֹרָה שֶׁהִיא נוֹתֶנֶת חַיִּים לְעֹשֶׂיהָ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וּבָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (משלי ד) כִּי חַיִּים הֵם לְמֹצְאֵיהֶם וּלְכָל בְּשָׂרוֹ מַרְפֵּא. וְאוֹמֵר (שם ג) רִפְאוּת תְּהִי לְשָׁרֶךָ וְשִׁקּוּי לְעַצְמוֹתֶיךָ. וְאוֹמֵר (שם ג) עֵץ חַיִּים הִיא לַמַּחֲזִיקִים בָּהּ וְתֹמְכֶיהָ מְאֻשָּׁר. וְאוֹמֵר (שם א) כִּי לִוְיַת חֵן הֵם לְרֹאשֶׁךָ וַעֲנָקִים לְגַרְגְּרֹתֶיךָ. וְאוֹמֵר (שם ד) תִּתֵּן לְרֹאשְׁךָ לִוְיַת חֵן עֲטֶרֶת תִּפְאֶרֶת תְּמַגְּנֶךָּ. וְאוֹמֵר (שם ט) כִּי בִי יִרְבּוּ יָמֶיךָ וְיוֹסִיפוּ לְךָ שְׁנוֹת חַיִּים. וְאוֹמֵר (שם ג) אֹרֶךְ יָמִים בִּימִינָהּ בִּשְׂמֹאולָהּ עֹשֶׁר וְכָבוֹד. וְאוֹמֵר (שם) כִּי אֹרֶךְ יָמִים וּשְׁנוֹת חַיִּים וְשָׁלוֹם יוֹסִיפוּ לָךְ. וְאוֹמֵר (שם) דְּרָכֶיהָ דַּרְכֵי נֹעַם וְכָל נְתִיבוֹתֶיהָ שָׁלוֹם:

### Translation under review (6:7)

English: Great is the Torah, in that it gives life to those who practice it in this world and in the world to come, as it is said: For they are life to those who find them, and healing to all his flesh. And it says: It shall be healing to your navel and refreshment to your bones. And it says: It is a tree of life to those who hold fast to it, and those who support it are happy. And it says: For they are a wreath of grace for your head and necklaces for your throat. And it says: She will give to your head a wreath of grace; a crown of splendor she will present to you. And it says: For through me your days will increase, and years of life will be added to you. And it says: Length of days is in her right hand; in her left hand, wealth and honor. And it says: For length of days and years of life and peace they will add to you. And it says: Her ways are ways of pleasantness, and all her paths are peace.

Literal: great Torah that-she giving life to-doers-of-her in-the world the-this and-in-the-world the-coming that-it-was-said (Proverbs 4) for life they to-finders-of-them and-to-all flesh-his healing and-says (ibid. 3) healing she-shall-be to-your-navel and-drink to-your-bones and-says (ibid. 3) tree-of life it to-the-holders in-her and-supporters-of-her happy and-says (ibid. 1) for wreath-of grace they to-your-head and-necklaces to-your-throat and-says (ibid. 4) she-will-give to-your-head wreath-of grace crown-of splendor she-will-present-you and-says (ibid. 9) for in-me they-will-increase your-days and-they-will-add to-you years-of life and-says (ibid. 3) length-of days in-her-right-hand in-her-left-hand wealth and-honor and-says (ibid.) for length-of days and-years-of life and-peace they-will-add to-you and-says (ibid.) her-ways ways-of pleasantness and-all her-paths peace

Grammar notes:
- גְּדוֹלָה תוֹרָה: the predicate adjective comes first, so the sentence reads "great is the Torah".
- שֶׁהִיא נוֹתֶנֶת: a participle in a שֶׁ-clause that gives the reason; לְעֹשֶׂיהָ is a participle with a feminine suffix, "to its doers".
- The chain of proof texts is joined by וְאוֹמֵר, "and it says", each introducing one more verse; שם "ibid." gives the book and the next token gives the chapter.
- In the quoted verses the feminine Torah (Wisdom in Proverbs) is the subject of תִּתֵּן, תְּמַגְּנֶךָּ and the suffixes -הּ, -הָ.
- וְיוֹסִיפוּ and יוֹסִיפוּ are third-person plural with no stated subject, so the English uses the passive.

Translator's note: The citations of chapter and book are left out of the reading text and given in the apparatus. Rendered literally, תִּפְאֶרֶת is "splendor" throughout the unit, and שָׁרֶךָ is "your navel" (the sense in Prov 3:8).

## Pirkei Avot 6:8

Hebrew (Torat Emet): רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן יְהוּדָה מִשּׁוּם רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן יוֹחַאי אוֹמֵר, הַנּוֹי וְהַכֹּחַ וְהָעֹשֶׁר וְהַכָּבוֹד וְהַחָכְמָה וְהַזִּקְנָה וְהַשֵּׂיבָה וְהַבָּנִים, נָאֶה לַצַּדִּיקִים וְנָאֶה לָעוֹלָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם טז) עֲטֶרֶת תִּפְאֶרֶת שֵׂיבָה בְּדֶרֶךְ צְדָקָה תִּמָּצֵא. וְאוֹמֵר (שם כ) תִּפְאֶרֶת בַּחוּרִים כֹּחָם וַהֲדַר זְקֵנִים שֵׂיבָה. וְאוֹמֵר (שם יד) עֲטֶרֶת חֲכָמִים עָשְׁרָם. וְאוֹמֵר (שם יז) עֲטֶרֶת זְקֵנִים בְּנֵי בָנִים וְתִפְאֶרֶת בָּנִים אֲבוֹתָם. וְאוֹמֵר (ישעיה כד) וְחָפְרָה הַלְּבָנָה וּבוֹשָׁה הַחַמָּה, כִּי מָלַךְ ה' צְבָאוֹת בְּהַר צִיּוֹן וּבִירוּשָׁלַיִם וְנֶגֶד זְקֵנָיו כָּבוֹד. רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן מְנַסְיָא אוֹמֵר, אֵלּוּ שֶׁבַע מִדּוֹת שֶׁמָּנוּ חֲכָמִים לַצַּדִּיקִים, כֻּלָּם נִתְקַיְּמוּ בְרַבִּי וּבְבָנָיו:

### Translation under review (6:8)

English: Rabbi Shimon son of Yehudah, in the name of Rabbi Shimon son of Yochai, says: Beauty, strength, wealth, honor, wisdom, old age, gray hair, and children are fitting for the righteous and fitting for the world, as it is said: Gray hair is a crown of splendor; in the way of righteousness it is found. And it says: The splendor of young men is their strength, and the glory of the old is gray hair. And it says: The crown of the wise is their wealth. And it says: The crown of the old is children's children, and the splendor of children is their fathers. And it says: The moon will be abashed and the sun ashamed, for the LORD of hosts reigns on Mount Zion and in Jerusalem, and before His elders is glory. Rabbi Shimon son of Menasya says: These seven qualities that the sages enumerated for the righteous, all of them were fulfilled in Rabbi and in his sons.

Literal: my-master Shimon son-of Yehudah on-account-of my-master Shimon son-of Yochai saying the-beauty and-the-strength and-the-wealth and-the-honor and-the-wisdom and-the-old-age and-the-gray-hair and-the-sons fitting to-the-righteous and-fitting to-the-world that-it-was-said (Proverbs 16) crown-of splendor gray-hair in-way-of righteousness she-is-found and-says (ibid. 20) splendor-of young-men their-strength and-glory-of elders gray-hair and-says (ibid. 14) crown-of wise-ones their-wealth and-says (ibid. 17) crown-of elders sons-of sons and-splendor-of sons their-fathers and-says (Isaiah 24) and-she-will-be-ashamed the-moon and-she-is-ashamed the-sun for he-reigned the-LORD of-hosts on-mount-of Zion and-in-Jerusalem and-before his-elders glory my-master Shimon son-of Menasya saying these seven qualities-of that-counted sages to-the-righteous all-of-them they-were-fulfilled in-my-master and-in-his-sons

Grammar notes:
- מִשּׁוּם introduces the tradition: "in the name of", literally "on account of".
- The eight terms of the list each take the article; נָאֶה is a singular adjective predicated of the whole list, "fitting".
- שֶׁמָּנוּ is a relative clause, "that they counted"; נִתְקַיְּמוּ is Nitpael, a late stem with reflexive-passive sense, "were fulfilled".
- The verses use poetic parallelism (crown // splendor); Isaiah 24:23 has חָפְרָה, from חפר "to be ashamed", and בוש.

Translator's note: The Hebrew list has eight terms (beauty, strength, wealth, honor, wisdom, old age, gray hair, children) while R. Shimon son of Menasya speaks of seven qualities; the difficulty is kept and not smoothed over. רַבִּי in the last clause is Rabbi Yehudah the Patriarch. The pack gives חָפַר "to dig" as the lemma of וְחָפְרָה; the English follows the verse, חפר "to be abashed" (a homograph).

- apparatus: Isaiah 24:23
- apparatus: Vilna omits some citation tokens.

## Pirkei Avot 6:9

Hebrew (Torat Emet): אָמַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בֶן קִסְמָא, פַּעַם אַחַת הָיִיתִי מְהַלֵּךְ בַּדֶּרֶךְ וּפָגַע בִּי אָדָם אֶחָד, וְנָתַן לִי שָׁלוֹם, וְהֶחֱזַרְתִּי לוֹ שָׁלוֹם. אָמַר לִי, רַבִּי, מֵאֵיזֶה מָקוֹם אַתָּה. אָמַרְתִּי לוֹ, מֵעִיר גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁל חֲכָמִים וְשֶׁל סוֹפְרִים אָנִי. אָמַר לִי, רַבִּי, רְצוֹנְךָ שֶׁתָּדוּר עִמָּנוּ בִמְקוֹמֵנוּ, וַאֲנִי אֶתֵּן לְךָ אֶלֶף אֲלָפִים דִּינְרֵי זָהָב וַאֲבָנִים טוֹבוֹת וּמַרְגָּלִיּוֹת. אָמַרְתִּי לוֹ, בְּנִי, אִם אַתָּה נוֹתֵן לִי כָל כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב וַאֲבָנִים טוֹבוֹת וּמַרְגָּלִיּוֹת שֶׁבָּעוֹלָם, אֵינִי דָר אֶלָּא בִמְקוֹם תּוֹרָה. וְלֹא עוֹד, אֶלָּא שֶׁבִּשְׁעַת פְּטִירָתוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם אֵין מְלַוִּין לוֹ לָאָדָם לֹא כֶסֶף וְלֹא זָהָב וְלֹא אֲבָנִים טוֹבוֹת וּמַרְגָּלִיּוֹת, אֶלָּא תוֹרָה וּמַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים בִּלְבַד, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (משלי ו) בְּהִתְהַלֶּכְךָ תַּנְחֶה אֹתָךְ, בְּשָׁכְבְּךָ תִּשְׁמֹר עָלֶיךָ, וַהֲקִיצוֹתָ הִיא תְשִׂיחֶךָ. בְּהִתְהַלֶּכְךָ תַּנְחֶה אֹתָךְ, בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, בְּשָׁכְבְּךָ תִּשְׁמֹר עָלֶיךָ, בַּקֶּבֶר, וַהֲקִיצוֹתָ הִיא תְשִׂיחֶךָ, לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא. וְכֵן כָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר תְּהִלִּים עַל יְדֵי דָוִד מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל (תהלים קיט), טוֹב לִי תוֹרַת פִּיךָ מֵאַלְפֵי זָהָב וָכָסֶף. וְאוֹמֵר (חגי ב) לִי הַכֶּסֶף וְלִי הַזָּהָב אָמַר ה' צְבָאוֹת:

### Translation under review (6:9)

English: Rabbi Yose son of Kisma said: Once I was walking on the road and a certain man met me, and he greeted me, and I returned his greeting. He said to me: Rabbi, from what place are you? I said to him: I am from a great city of sages and of scribes. He said to me: Rabbi, is it your wish to dwell with us in our place? And I will give you a thousand thousand dinars of gold and precious stones and pearls. I said to him: My son, if you give me all the silver and gold and precious stones and pearls that are in the world, I dwell nowhere except in a place of Torah. And not only that, but at the hour of a person's departure nothing accompanies a person: neither silver nor gold nor precious stones or pearls, but only Torah and good deeds. as it is said: When you walk, it will lead you; when you lie down, it will watch over you; and when you awake, it will talk with you. When you walk, it will lead you in this world; when you lie down, it will watch over you in the grave; and when you awake, it will talk with you in the world to come. And so it is written in the Book of Psalms by the hand of David, king of Israel: The Torah of Your mouth is better to me than thousands of gold and silver. And it says: Mine is the silver and mine is the gold, says the LORD of hosts.

Grammar notes:
- The story is told in the first person with a perfect plus participle (הָיִיתִי מְהַלֵּךְ), "I was walking".
- נָתַן שָׁלוֹם is an idiom for giving a greeting; הֶחֱזִיר שָׁלוֹם, "returned a greeting".
- אֵינִי דָר אֶלָּא "I dwell only/nowhere but" is a negative with אֶלָּא: exclusive sense.
- אֵין מְלַוִּין is a plural participle with no stated subject (impersonal), followed by a list governed by לֹא ... וְלֹא.
- The verse is quoted twice: first whole, then split into its three clauses, each tied to a world (this world, the grave, the world to come).
- מֵאַלְפֵי זָהָב וָכָסֶף: "than thousands of gold and silver", with an understood unit of coin.

Translator's note: מֵעִיר גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁל חֲכָמִים: the particle שֶׁל is mishnaic; the dinars of gold are dinars (דִּינָר). The sentence about what accompanies a person is impersonal in Hebrew ("they do not accompany him"); the English gives the sense of the plural participle without a subject.

Commentary rulings:
- apparatus: Proverbs 6:22
- apparatus: Psalms 119:72
- apparatus: Haggai 2:8
- apparatus: Vilna omits some citation tokens (משלי ו, תהלים קיט, חגי ב).

## Pirkei Avot 6:10

Hebrew (Torat Emet): חֲמִשָּׁה קִנְיָנִים קָנָה לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְעוֹלָמוֹ, וְאֵלּוּ הֵן, תּוֹרָה קִנְיָן אֶחָד, שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ קִנְיָן אֶחָד, אַבְרָהָם קִנְיָן אֶחָד, יִשְׂרָאֵל קִנְיָן אֶחָד, בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קִנְיָן אֶחָד. תּוֹרָה מִנַּיִן, דִּכְתִיב (משלי ח), ה' קָנָנִי רֵאשִׁית דַּרְכּוֹ קֶדֶם מִפְעָלָיו מֵאָז. שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ קִנְיָן אֶחָד מִנַּיִן, דִּכְתִיב (ישעיה סו), כֹּה אָמַר ה' הַשָּׁמַיִם כִּסְאִי וְהָאָרֶץ הֲדֹם רַגְלָי אֵי זֶה בַיִת אֲשֶׁר תִּבְנוּ לִי וְאֵי זֶה מָקוֹם מְנוּחָתִי, וְאוֹמֵר (תהלים קד) מָה רַבּוּ מַעֲשֶׂיךָ ה' כֻּלָּם בְּחָכְמָה עָשִׂיתָ מָלְאָה הָאָרֶץ קִנְיָנֶךָ. אַבְרָהָם קִנְיָן אֶחָד מִנַּיִן, דִּכְתִיב (בראשית יד), וַיְבָרְכֵהוּ וַיֹּאמַר בָּרוּךְ אַבְרָם לְאֵל עֶלְיוֹן קֹנֵה שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ. יִשְׂרָאֵל קִנְיָן אֶחָד מִנַּיִן, דִּכְתִיב (שמות טו), עַד יַעֲבֹר עַמְּךָ ה' עַד יַעֲבֹר עַם זוּ קָנִיתָ, וְאוֹמֵר (תהלים טז) לִקְדוֹשִׁים אֲשֶׁר בָּאָרֶץ הֵמָּה וְאַדִּירֵי כָּל חֶפְצִי בָם. בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קִנְיָן אֶחָד מִנַּיִן, דִּכְתִיב (שמות טו), מָכוֹן לְשִׁבְתְּךָ פָּעַלְתָּ ה' מִקְּדָשׁ ה' כּוֹנְנוּ יָדֶיךָ. וְאוֹמֵר (תהלים עח) וַיְבִיאֵם אֶל גְּבוּל קָדְשׁוֹ הַר זֶה קָנְתָה יְמִינוֹ:

### Translation under review (6:10)

English: Five possessions the Holy One, blessed be He, acquired for Himself in His world, and these are they: Torah, one possession; heaven and earth, one possession; Abraham, one possession; Israel, one possession; the Temple, one possession. Torah: from where do we know it? For it is written The LORD acquired me as the beginning of His way, before His works of old. Heaven and earth, one possession: from where do we know it? For it is written Thus says the LORD: The heavens are my throne and the earth is My footstool; what house is it that you would build for Me and what is the place of My rest? And it says: How many are Your works, O LORD; You made them all in wisdom; the earth is full of Your possessions. Abraham, one possession: from where do we know it? For it is written And he blessed him and said: Blessed be Abram to God Most High, possessor of heaven and earth. Israel, one possession: from where do we know it? For it is written Until Your people pass over, O LORD, until the people You acquired pass over. And it says: As for the holy ones who are in the land, they, and the majestic ones, all my delight is in them. The Temple, one possession: from where do we know it? For it is written The place for Your dwelling You made, O LORD; the sanctuary, O LORD, Your hands established. And it says: And He brought them to the border of His holiness, this mountain that His right hand acquired.

Literal: five possessions acquired for-him the-Holy-One blessed He in-his-world and-these they Torah possession one heavens and-earth possession one Abraham possession one Israel possession one house-of the-sanctuary possession one Torah from-where that-it-is-written (Proverbs 8) the-LORD he-acquired-me beginning-of his-way before his-works from-of-old heavens and-earth possession one from-where that-it-is-written (Isaiah 66) thus said the-LORD the-heavens my-throne and-the-earth footstool-of my-feet which this house that you-will-build for-me and-which this place my-rest and-says (Psalms 104) how are-many your-works LORD all-of-them in-wisdom you-made she-is-full the-earth your-possessions Abraham possession one from-where that-it-is-written (Genesis 14) and-he-blessed-him and-he-said blessed Abram to-God Most-High acquirer-of heavens and-earth Israel possession one from-where that-it-is-written (Exodus 15) until he-passes your-people LORD until he-passes people this you-acquired and-says (Psalms 16) to-holy-ones that in-the-land they and-majestic-of all my-delight in-them house-of the-sanctuary possession one from-where that-it-is-written (Exodus 15) foundation for-your-dwelling you-made LORD sanctuary LORD they-established your-hands and-says (Psalms 78) and-he-brought-them to border-of his-holiness mountain this she-acquired his-right-hand

Grammar notes:
- קִנְיָן is a noun of the form קִנְיָן "acquisition, possession", from קנה; the mishnah reads the verb קנה in each verse as "acquire".
- מִנַּיִן is the formula that asks for the proof text: "from where [do we learn it]?"; דִּכְתִיב "for it is written" introduces the answer.
- וְאֵלּוּ הֵן, "and these are they", introduces the list.
- Heaven and earth form one possession: שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ is the paired phrase, with both nouns under one קִנְיָן אֶחָד.
- In the verses, qal participle קֹנֵה (construct) governs שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ; Exodus 15:16 repeats עַד יַעֲבֹר and זוּ is a relative pronoun.

Translator's note: In Prov 8:22 קָנָנִי may also mean "created me"; the mishnah reads "acquired", and the English follows it. For Gen 14:19 קֹנֵה may be "maker" or "possessor"; "possessor" matches the point of the mishnah. The pack marks לְאֵל (Gen 14:19) and אֶל (Ps 78:54) with the lemma אַל; the data is kept, and the words are the noun "God" and the preposition "to".

- apparatus: Vilna adds קנין אחד at one point where the base has none.

## Pirkei Avot 6:11

Hebrew (Torat Emet): כָּל מַה שֶּׁבָּרָא הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּעוֹלָמוֹ, לֹא בְרָאוֹ אֶלָּא לִכְבוֹדוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ישעיה מג), כֹּל הַנִּקְרָא בִשְׁמִי וְלִכְבוֹדִי בְּרָאתִיו יְצַרְתִּיו אַף עֲשִׂיתִיו, וְאוֹמֵר (שמות טו), יְהֹוָה יִמְלֹךְ לְעֹלָם וָעֶד. רַבִּי חֲנַנְיָא בֶּן עֲקַשְׁיָא אוֹמֵר, רָצָה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְזַכּוֹת אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, לְפִיכָךְ הִרְבָּה לָהֶם תּוֹרָה וּמִצְוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ישעיה מב) ה' חָפֵץ לְמַעַן צִדְקוֹ יַגְדִּיל תּוֹרָה וְיַאְדִּיר:

### Translation under review (6:11)

English: Everything that the Holy One, blessed be He, created in His world, He did not create it except for His glory, as it is said: Everyone who is called by My name and for My glory I created him, I formed him, indeed I made him. And it says: The LORD will reign forever and ever. Rabbi Hananya son of Akashya says: The Holy One, blessed be He, wished to grant merit to Israel; therefore He gave them abundant Torah and commandments, as it is said: The LORD was pleased for the sake of His righteousness to magnify Torah and make it glorious.

Literal: all what that-created the-Holy-One blessed He in-his-world not he-created-it except for-his-glory that-it-was-said (Isaiah 43) all the-called by-my-name and-for-my-glory I-created-him I-formed-him also I-made-him and-says (Exodus 15) the-LORD he-will-reign for-eternity and-ever my-master Hananya son-of Akashya saying he-wished the-Holy-One blessed He to-grant-merit object-marker Israel therefore he-multiplied to-them Torah and-commandments that-it-was-said (Isaiah 42) the-LORD he-was-pleased for-the-sake-of his-righteousness he-will-magnify Torah and-he-will-make-glorious

- 6:11.48 6:11.49 | - | (Isaiah 42)
- 6:11.50 6:11.51 | The LORD was pleased | the-LORD he-was-pleased
- 6:11.52 6:11.53 | for the sake of His righteousness | for-the-sake-of his-righteousness
- 6:11.54 6:11.55 | to magnify Torah | he-will-magnify Torah
- 6:11.56 | and make it glorious. | and-he-will-make-glorious

Grammar notes:
- כָּל מַה שֶּׁ- "everything that": כָּל plus the indefinite relative מַה, then the relative שֶׁ.
- לֹא ... אֶלָּא "not ... except": "He created it only for His glory".
- In Isaiah 43:7 three verbs in the perfect (בְּרָאתִיו, יְצַרְתִּיו, עֲשִׂיתִיו) with a pronoun suffix form a rising series: created, formed, made.
- לְזַכּוֹת is the infinitive of זכה in the Hifil sense "to give merit to, to make worthy".
- In Isaiah 42:21 the imperfect יַגְדִּיל ... וְיַאְדִּיר is read as the purpose of חָפֵץ: "to magnify ... and make glorious".

Translator's note: In Exodus 15:18 the divine name is written with vowels in the pack; English renders "the LORD". In Isaiah 42:21, לְמַעַן צִדְקוֹ is "for the sake of His righteousness" and the verse is quoted in the form of the mishnah.

Commentary rulings:
- apparatus: Isaiah 43:7
- apparatus: Exodus 15:18
- apparatus: Isaiah 42:21
- apparatus: Vilna reads אמר where the base has no word at one point.

- `1.5` Hophal to be laid

### `bdbs:שָׁלוֹם` שָׁלוֹם (n-m): completeness, soundness, welfare, peace
- `1` completeness, soundness, welfare, peace
- `1.1` completeness (in number)
- `1.2` safety, soundness (in body)
- `1.3` welfare, health, prosperity
- `1.4` peace, quiet, tranquillity, contentment
- `1.5` peace, friendship

- `1.5.2` with God especially in covenant relationship
- `1.6` peace (from war)
- `1.7` peace (as adjective)

### `bdbs:שָׁמַיִם` שָׁמַיִם (n-m): heaven, heavens, sky

- `1.1.2` as the visible universe, the sky, atmosphere, etc
- `1.2` Heaven (as the abode of God)

### `bdbs:שָׁמַר` שָׁמַר (v): to keep, guard, observe, give heed

- `1.1.2` Qal to keep, guard, keep watch and ward, protect, save life

- `1.1.3` Qal to watch for, wait for
- `1.1.4` Qal to watch, observe
- `1.1.5` Qal to keep, retain, treasure up (in memory)
- `1.1.6` Qal to keep (within bounds), restrain
- `1.1.7` Qal to observe, celebrate, keep (sabbath or covenant or commands), perform (vow)
- `1.1.8` Qal to keep, preserve, protect
- `1.1.9` Qal to keep, reserve
- `1.2.1` Niphal to be on one's guard, take heed, take care, beware
- `1.2.2` Niphal to keep oneself, refrain, abstain
- `1.2.3` Niphal to be kept, be guarded
- `1.3` Piel to keep, pay heed
- `1.4` Hithpael to keep oneself from

- `1.1` as division of time
- `1.2` as measure of time
- `1.3` as indication of age
- `1.4` a lifetime (of years of life)

- `1.3` Piel to change, alter

### `jastrow:אָב II` אָב II (m.): , const. אֲבִי, אַב [embracer], father, ancestor, progenitor; teacher; chief, leader; author, originator. Ex. R. s. 46 end המגדל אב the educator is the real father. Lev. R. s. 1 אבי החכמה וכ׳, the father of all wisdom, … the father of prophets. Y. Ned. V, 39ᵇ;…
- `1` , const. אֲבִי, אַב [embracer], father, ancestor, progenitor; teacher; chief, leader; author, originator. Ex. R. s. 46 end המגדל אב the educator is the real father. Lev. R. s. 1 אבי החכמה וכ׳, the father of all wisdom, … the father of prophets. Y. Ned. V, 39ᵇ;…
- `2` fathers, ancestors, patriarchs &c. Ber. 26ᵇ prayers א׳ תקנום have been instituted by the Patriarchs; a. fr.—אבות בתי דינין, v. supra. Ḥag. II, 2.—בת א׳ a woman of noble descent. Num. R. s. 1; a. e.—Metaph. principal, chief א׳ מלאכות, v. supra. א׳ הטומא׳ Kel. I…
- `3` Aboth, the first section of the Prayer of Benedictions (v. תְּפִלָּה), so called because it alludes to the Patriarchs. R. Hash. IV, 5; a. fr.
- `4` Aboth, name of a treatise of the Mishnah, containing sayings of Talmudic authorities and belonging to the fourth section, נזיקין, of the Mishnah collection (משניות); also styled פִּרְקֵי א׳ a. מְסֶכֶת א׳. A similar collection of a later date is contained in Ta…

### `jastrow:אָב II ²` אָב II ²: ch., v. אַבָּא
- `1` ch., v. אַבָּא

### `jastrow:אָדַר` אָדַר: to cut off, surround, isolate; whence
- `1` to cut off, surround, isolate; whence
- `2` (b. h.) to distinguish.—Den. אַדִּיר.
- `3` (Assyr. v. אֲדָר) to darken.
- `4` *to strip. cmp. עדר.—B. Kam. 11ᵃ (ref. to עד Ex. XXII, 12, v. עַד in H. Dict.) יביא אֲדוּרָה לב"ד Ar. (ed. עֲדוּדָה, v. עדד; Ms. ארורה, corr. acc.) let him bring the stripped (the remnants of the torn animal, skin &c.) before court for assessment of damages.

- `1` m. (b. h.) man, pr. n. m. Adam, frequ. א׳ הראשון (abbr. אה"ר). Gen. R. s. 17; a. fr.—ספרו של, ספרא דאה"ר אה"ר the (allegorical) book of Adam containing all generations and their leaders from beginning to resurrection, i.e. destinies of humanity. Ex. R. s. 40 b…

- `1` (a) to join, knot; to be knotted, thick; b) to heap up; c) transf. to join words, compose, cmp. דבר; d) to contract, bargain, exchange. [As to Assyr. to see, cmp. חמא.])
- `2` to speak, think, say, relate &c. … א׳ ר׳ … א׳ ר׳ Rabbi … related in the name of R. … Ber. 3ᵇ; a. v. fr.—Part. pass. אָמוּר expression. Yoma 70ᵇ, a. fr. הוא הא׳ וכ׳ it is the same expression (‘one ram’) used here and in the Book of Numbers. Sifré Deut. s. 334, …
- `3` to vow, devote. אָמוּר (לגבוה) devoted to the Lord. Succ. 55ᵇ; v. אֲמִירָה I, 3, a. אֵימוּרִים.
- `4.1` Nif. (b. h.) to be said, to read … נא׳ כאן … ונ׳ להלן we read here … and we read there … (the same expression is used here and there in the Scriptural text). Ber. 9ᵃ; a. v. fr.—שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (abbr. שנ׳, שנא׳) for it is said in the Scriptures (as evidence in favor of …

### `jastrow:אָמַר II` אָמַר II: , v. אָמַר I, a) to be thick, strong.
- `1` , v. אָמַר I, a) to be thick, strong.
- `2.1` Hif. to thrive; to boast, vaunt, be oppressive (cmp. Ps. XCIV, 4). Sot. IX, 15 (49ᵇ) יוקר יַאֲמִיר the nobility shall be oppressive (Snh. 97ᵃ יְעַוֵּת; Der. Er. Zut. X יעוות; Cant. R. to II, 13 הווה).—*[
- `2.2` Hif. (denom. of מריא to fatten. Sabb. XXIV, 3 (155ᵇ) Bab. מַאֲמִירִין, Ms. Oxf., Mish. a. Y. מַמְרִים, מַמְרִין, v. מרי.]

XXVIII, 29; a. e.—מִבְּ׳ from the beginning. Ib. XLI, 4.—מעשה ב׳ a) creation. Gen. R. s. 3; a. fr.—b) cosmogony, contrad. to מעשה מרכבה …
- `1` (b. h.) in the beginning, as a cosmological term (ref. to Gen. I, 1) creation, primeval period, Nature, Universe. Targ. Is. XXVIII, 29; a. e.—מִבְּ׳ from the beginning. Ib. XLI, 4.—מעשה ב׳ a) creation. Gen. R. s. 3; a. fr.—b) cosmogony, contrad. to מעשה מרכבה …

### `jastrow:בֵּן` בֵּן (m.): , constr. בֶּן (b. h.; בנה) offspring, son, child. שבוע הב׳ the male child’s week, a disguise for circumcision day, adopted during the Hadrianic persecutions. Snh. 32ᵇ; Y. Keth. I, 25ᶜ; a. e.—ישוע הב׳ a disguise for פִּדְיוֹן הבן. B. Kam. 80ᵃ.—… בְּנוֹ של the …
- `1` , constr. בֶּן (b. h.; בנה) offspring, son, child. שבוע הב׳ the male child’s week, a disguise for circumcision day, adopted during the Hadrianic persecutions. Snh. 32ᵇ; Y. Keth. I, 25ᶜ; a. e.—ישוע הב׳ a disguise for פִּדְיוֹן הבן. B. Kam. 80ᵃ.—… בְּנוֹ של the …

### `jastrow:בַּיִת` בַּיִת (m.): cmp. preced.; v. Ges. H. Dict. s. v. as to various etymological attempts), constr. בֵּית, pl. בָּתִּים.
- `1` cmp. preced.; v. Ges. H. Dict. s. v. as to various etymological attempts), constr. בֵּית, pl. בָּתִּים.
- `2` house, household, home. Yoma 11ᵇ ב׳ מיוחד לדירה bayith means a building intended for a dwelling. Ib. מי שמייחד ביתו לו (Var. v. Rabb. D. S. a. l. note) he who devotes his house (its contents) to himself exclusively (unaccommodating); Arakh. 16ᵃ מי שמיוחד וכ׳.—…
- `3` Esp. (הַ)בַּ׳ the Temple. בפני הב׳ in days when the Temple exists, שלא בפני הב׳ when it does not exist. Ḥull. V, 1; a. fr.—ב׳ שני or ב׳ אחרון the Second Temple. Cant. R. to VIII, 9; a. fr.—הר הב׳ Temple Mount, v. הַר.
- `4` school, college, (collect.) disciples; בֵּית הלל Hillelites &c. Bets. I, 1; a. fr. Treat. Sof’rim IV, 1 של ב׳ וכ׳ those of the house of &c. [Y. Shebi. II, 33ᵈ bot., a. e. דבית … ר׳, usu. דבי, v. בֵּי].
- `5` body. Ber. 44ᵇ אוי לו לב׳ וכ׳ that human body (Rashi: stomach) is to be pitied where vegetables are a constant guest (being the only food).
- `6` wife. Yoma I, 1 ביתו זו וכ׳ ‘his house’—that means his wife. Ib. 13ᵃ והך לאו ביתו היא but this one (designated for him in case of his wife’s death) cannot be called ‘his house’. Sabb. 118ᵇ; a. fr.
- `7` Euphem. pudenda; marital intercourse. Y. Sabb. IX, 12ᵃ top; Mikv. VIII, 4 שמשה את בֵּיתָהּ she had intercourse. Ib. כבדה את הב׳ she washed &c. Y. Sot. I, 16ᶜ bot. אסורה לביתה is forbidden to her husband. Nidd. 5ᵃ מהומת לביתה she hastens to perform her marital …
- `8` store-house, store-room. בֵּית העצים wood-room; ב׳ התבן straw-magazine; ב׳ הבקר stable; &c. Yoma 11ᵃ; a. fr.
- `9` (geogr.) place, town, in compounds (for which see the respective determinants), e.g. ב׳ בוקיא Beth-Bukya &c.
- `10` (anat.) limb, organ, in compounds (v. supra 8)), e.g. ב׳ הבליעה œsophagus, &c.
- `11` shed for plants, covering. Shebi. II, 4 (pl.). Y. ib. 33ᵈ.—Y. Sabb. VII, 10ᵃ.—1
- `12` receptacle, cover &c., e.g. ב׳ הדיו inkstand. Tosef. Kel. B. Mets. IV, 11; ב׳ יד sleeve, v. אוּנְקְלַי II, &c.—Men. 34ᵇ, a. e. cases of the T’fillin
- `13.1` Chief compounds:. , pl. בָּתֵּי אָבוֹת
- `13.2` Chief compounds:. paternal home, family. Snh. 38ᵃ שני ב׳ א׳ two families (dynasties); a. fr.—Tosef. Ter. II, 11 בתי אבות = אריסי ב׳ א׳ hereditary land-tenants; B. Bath. 46ᵇ
- `13.3` Chief compounds:. priest’s division. Taan. II, 6; a. fr.; v. אֱנוֹשׁ pl.
- `13.4` Chief compounds:. origin of a law, rule &c.; cmp. אָב II. Cant. R. to II, 4 ב׳ א׳ של הלכה the origin (principle) of a legal rule. Midr. Till. to Ps. CIV, 29 wherever the root רעש is used, it means cessation of government, ואיזהו ב׳ א׳ שלהם וכ׳ and where is the origin (determini…

### `jastrow:בָּא I` בָּא I (m.): (= אַבָּא)
- `1` (= אַבָּא)
- `2` father. Targ. O. Num. III, 24; 30 רב בית בא (and through the entire chapter ed. Berl.; oth. ed. אבא).
- `3` pr. n. m. Ba = Abba. Y. Ber. I, 2ᶜ top (interchanging with אבא); a. v. fr. in Y., v. אַבָּא II.

### `jastrow:בָּא III` בָּא III (m.): (part. of בֹּוא) coming, future. עולם הַבָּא (abbr. עו"הב) the world to come, futurity. Snh. X, 1; a. fr.—לְהַבָּא (Bab.), לַבָּא (Y.) in future. מכאן ול׳ (מיכן) from this date and further, opp. למפרע retrospectively, or מעיקרא in the past. Pes. 7ᵇ לה׳ משמע it…
- `1` (part. of בֹּוא) coming, future. עולם הַבָּא (abbr. עו"הב) the world to come, futurity. Snh. X, 1; a. fr.—לְהַבָּא (Bab.), לַבָּא (Y.) in future. מכאן ול׳ (מיכן) from this date and further, opp. למפרע retrospectively, or מעיקרא in the past. Pes. 7ᵇ לה׳ משמע it…

### `jastrow:בָּחוּר` בָּחוּר (m.): chosen. Sabb. 105ᵃ (ref. to א̇ב̇, Gen. XVII, 5) I have made thee א̇ב a father of the nations, ב̇' נתתיך וכ׳ I have made thee a chosen one among the nations. Gen. R. s. 76, beg.; a. fr.
- `1` chosen. Sabb. 105ᵃ (ref. to א̇ב̇, Gen. XVII, 5) I have made thee א̇ב a father of the nations, ב̇' נתתיך וכ׳ I have made thee a chosen one among the nations. Gen. R. s. 76, beg.; a. fr.
- `2` young man, youth, unmarried. Keth. 7ᵇ, opp. אַלְמוֹן.—Gen. R. s. 39 ב׳ ובתולה מיכן a young man and a virgin on the one side (of the coin). Ḥag. 14ᵃ; a. fr.—Fem. בַּחוּרָה. Gen. R. s. 71; a. e.—Pl. בַּחוּרִים, f. בַּחוּרוֹת. Num. R. s. 10, beg. לב׳ קול וכ׳ to t…

### `jastrow:בָּרַךְ` בָּרַךְ: to cave out. Denom. בְּרֵכָה, בֶּרֶךְ; cmp. בּוּרְכָּא, בּוּרְכְּתָא.
- `1` to cave out. Denom. בְּרֵכָה, בֶּרֶךְ; cmp. בּוּרְכָּא, בּוּרְכְּתָא.
- `2` (cmp. ברר) to select, point out (cmp. esp. Gen. II, 3) whence to bless (Pi.).—Part. pass. בָּרוּךְ chosen, blessed, praised. הקדוש ב׳ הוא (abbr. הקב"ה) the Holy One, blessed be He. Pes. 118ᵃ; a. v. fr. Ib. 104ᵇ, a. fr. פותח בב׳ וכ׳ he (who prays) opens the ben…
- `3.1` Pi. (b. h.)
- `3.2` Pi. to praise, bless, esp. to recite the due benediction. Ber. VI, 1. Ib. 5 בי׳ על היין having recited the blessing over wine &c. Pes. X, 9 ב׳ ברכת הפסח having recited the blessing over the Paschal lamb. Ib. 5 אנחנו חייבים … לְבָרֵךְ we are bound to … praise &c.; …
- `3.3` Pi. (cmp. נקב) to blaspheme. Snh. 56ᵃ עד שֶיְבָרֵךְ שם בשם until he blasphemes the Lord by His name. Ib. שבי׳ את השם בכינוי who blasphemed the Lord by an attribute.—Part. Pu. מְבוֹרָךְ blessed, praiseworthy. Ber. VII, 3; a. fr.
- `3.4` Pi. *to cut through, to clear virgin ground or forest. Tosef. Shebi. III, 20 ed. Zuck. (Var. מגדר); Y. ib. IV, 35ᵇ bot. מבדין; Y. Sabb. VII, 10ᵃ top המברה, v. בהי h. [Tosef. Shebi. I, 6 מבורכות, v. infra.]
- `4.1` Hithpa. , Nithpa. נִתְבָּרֵךְ to be blessed (praised); to be increased (v. בְּרָכָה). Y. Ber. IX, 14ᵃ top ויִתְבָּרַךְ and be blessed. Y. M. Kat. I, beg. 80ᵃ if the waters were scanty ונִתְבָּרְכוּ and grew plentiful. Yeb. 63ᵃ; a. fr.
- `5.1` Hif. (denom. of בֶּרֶךְ or בְּרִיכָה) to form a knee, to engraft; esp. to bend a vine by drawing it into the ground and making it grow forth as an independent plant, to sink. Shebi. II, 6; R. Hash. 10ᵇ; a. fr.—Tosef. Shebi. I, 6 אם היו מוּבְרָכוֹת (incorr. מבורכ׳) …

### `jastrow:בּוֹשׁ` בּוֹשׁ: , v. בְּהַת); (as adj.) m. confounded, abashed, ashamed. Zeb. 101ᵃ הודה ולא ביש וכ׳ he confessed and was not ashamed so as to say, ‘I have not learned it’, but he said &c.; ib.ᵇ (v. Rabb. D. S. a. l.).—Ḥag. 22ᵇ בּוֹשְׁנִי = בוש אני; Tosef. Ohol. V, 11 בּוֹשְׁת…
- `1` , v. בְּהַת); (as adj.) m. confounded, abashed, ashamed. Zeb. 101ᵃ הודה ולא ביש וכ׳ he confessed and was not ashamed so as to say, ‘I have not learned it’, but he said &c.; ib.ᵇ (v. Rabb. D. S. a. l.).—Ḥag. 22ᵇ בּוֹשְׁנִי = בוש אני; Tosef. Ohol. V, 11 בּוֹשְׁת…
- `2.1` Pi. to put to shame, insult, disgrace. B. Kam. VIII, 1 המְבַיֵּישׁ את הישן he who exposes a sleeping person to shame. Ib. הזיק וב׳ he injured and exposed (a person at the same time). Ib. הכל לפי המְבַ׳ והמתבייש all (the fine) according to the social position of th…
- `3.1` *. (cmp. הוֹבִיר fr. בור) to trouble, spoil. Tosef. B. Kam. V, 12 ed. Zuck. (Var. הבאיש, v. בָּאַשׁ, as Mish. ib. V, 3).
- `4.1` Hithpa. a. Nithpa. to be put to shame, be exposed, insulted; to be bashful. B. Kam. l. c. Ned. 20ᵃ. Num. R. s. 15 התב׳ והטמין he felt ashamed (to offer the king common accommodations) and hid &c. Ib. נִתְבַּיַּישְׁתִּי; a. fr.

### `jastrow:גְּבוּל I` גְּבוּל I: [heap, mound,]
- `1` [heap, mound,]
- `2` landmark, boundary; limit; qualification. B. Bath. 69ᵇ (ref. to Gen. XXIII, 17) מי שצריך לג׳ סביב such trees as require boundaries (small trees, are included in the sale). Ab. Zar. 24ᵇ ג׳ יש לה this assertion (לוקחין מהן בהמה לקרבן, ib. 23ᵇ) must be qualified.…
- `3` (in gen.) country, contrad. to the sanctuary (מקדש) and Jerusalem. Keth. 24ᵇ, a. fr. קדשי הג׳ the sacred gifts (T’rumah &c.) set apart and consumed outside of the Temple and Jerusalem.—Pl. גְּבוּלִים, גְּבוּלִין. B. Bath. 56ᵃ עיירות העומדות על הג׳ border-towns…

R. s. 24 the Lord created for man מעין בתוך גַּרְגַּרְתֹּו a well (mucous membranes) in the trachea. Ḥull. II, 4 פס…
- `1` pl. גַּרְגְּרוֹת neck; גרגר; v. גָּרַר, throat, gullet; (in ritual law) wind-pipe, trachea. Koh. R. to XII, 6; Lev. R. s. 18, v. גָּלָה Hif.—Ex. R. s. 24 the Lord created for man מעין בתוך גַּרְגַּרְתֹּו a well (mucous membranes) in the trachea. Ḥull. II, 4 פס…

### `jastrow:גָּדַל I` גָּדַל I: v. גדל II) to be high, to grow, be large, tall. Ex. R. s. 1 שהיה גָדֵל שלא וכ׳ he was extraordinarily tall for his age. Ib. וכי אין הכל גְּדֵילִים do not all children grow?—Y. Maasr. I, 49ᵃ, v. טְפוּס; a. fr. Fem. גְּדֵילָה, pl. גְּדֵילוֹת. Succ. 34ᵃ; a. fr.
- `1` v. גדל II) to be high, to grow, be large, tall. Ex. R. s. 1 שהיה גָדֵל שלא וכ׳ he was extraordinarily tall for his age. Ib. וכי אין הכל גְּדֵילִים do not all children grow?—Y. Maasr. I, 49ᵃ, v. טְפוּס; a. fr. Fem. גְּדֵילָה, pl. גְּדֵילוֹת. Succ. 34ᵃ; a. fr.
- `2.1` Pi. to raise (of live stock and of plants); to rear, train. Kil. VIII, 1 מותרים לגַדֵּל you are permitted to raise. Snh. 19ᵇ מיכל גִּידְּלָה Michal reared (Mirab’s children). Ib.; Meg. 13ᵃ המְגַדֵּל יתום וכ׳ he who educates an orphan in his house. Gen. R. s. 98 הי…
- `2.2` Pi. to raise to dignity, make famous; to praise. Hor. 9ᵃ (ref. to Lev. XXI, 10) whence do we know אם אין לו שחייבין לגַדְּלוֹ when he (the Highpriest) is poor, that they (the brethren) are bound to raise him (make him independent)? Ib. גַּדְּלֵהוּ משל אחיו raise h…
- `3.1` Hif. to grow up, to become of age. Yeb. X, 9 וּמִשֶּׁהִ׳ and after he is of age. Ib. XIII, 1 עד שתַּגְדִּיל until she becomes of age; a. fr.
- `4.1` Hithpa. , Nithpa. נִתְגַּדֵּל
- `4.2` Hithpa. to be raised to dignity. Esth. R. to III, 1 יִתְגַּדֵּל ואח"כ יתלה let him first become great and then be hanged. Gen. R. s. 99, end (play on פֹּרָת, Gen. XLIX, 22) ע"י פָּרוֹת נ׳ by means of cows (Pharaoh’s dream) was he raised to power; a. fr.— to be magnifi…
- `4.3` Hithpa. to glorify one’s self, to boast, parade. Ned. 62ᵃ; Ab. IV, 5 אל תעשם עטרה להִתְגַּדֵּל בהם make them (the words of the Law) not a crown to parade therewith.
- `4.4` Hithpa. to grow, prosper, be nursed. Tanḥ. V’zoth 1; Pesik. ib. p. 199ᵃ הרי … מִתְגַּדֵּל עמו the poison-bearing tree will be nursed along with it (the health-giving tree). Tanḥ. B’resh. 7 נִתְגַּדַּלְתָּ וחטאת thou didst grow older and didst sin, opp. תינוק היית; a. …

### `jastrow:דָּר ³` דָּר ³: same, row, range, order. Keth. 60ᵃ בד׳ דנשי in a row of women. B. Kam. 117ᵃ בד׳ קמא in the first row of scholars. Ḥull. 11ᵃ top ד׳ דגברי a row of men. Ib. 47ᵃ בד׳ דאוני within the ranges of the lobes of the lungs. Ib. 53ᵃ בד׳ דסיחופיה in the order in which the…
- `1` same, row, range, order. Keth. 60ᵃ בד׳ דנשי in a row of women. B. Kam. 117ᵃ בד׳ קמא in the first row of scholars. Ḥull. 11ᵃ top ד׳ דגברי a row of men. Ib. 47ᵃ בד׳ דאוני within the ranges of the lobes of the lungs. Ib. 53ᵃ בד׳ דסיחופיה in the order in which the…
- `2` a range of wood, pyre. Cant. R. to III, 4 ד׳ יקידא יקדת a burning pyre hast thou set on fire, v. יְקַד.—Pl. as above. Targ. Ps. LXXXII, 15 Ms. (ed. זרזי).
- `3` (v. דּוֹר) period, generation. Targ. Deut. XXXIII, 7. Targ. Job. VI, 17; a. fr.—Ḥag. 5ᵃ עד דמלי להו לד׳ until they have completed the period (lived the years allotted to them). Snh. 97ᵇ בכל ד׳ וד׳ (Ms. M.) in each generation; a. fr.—Pl. דָּרִין, דָּרַיָּא, דָּ…

- `3` (introducing a question or exclamation) how?, indeed! Y. Shek. I, 45ᵈ bot. הן נקרא ולא וכ׳ can we, indeed, read this and not feel ashamed? (Bab. ed. הניקרא, marginal correct. Ms. M. הן נק׳).
- `4` if. Dan. II, 5, sq.; a. fr.—הן—הן whether—or. Ezra VII, 26.—B. Bath. VII, 2 הן חסר הן יתר whether it be less (than a Beth Kor) or more. Ib. 3; a. e.
- `5` yes (cmp. אִין I). B. Mets. 49ᵃ, v. הִין II Mekh. Yithro s. 4 answer על לאו לאו ועל הן הן no to a prohibition and yes to a positive command. Ib. s. 5 ה׳ וה׳ yes, indeed; a fr.—Ned. 11ᵃ, a. fr. מכלל לאו אתה שומע הן from the negative we derive the affirmative by…

- `1` (b. h.) to go, go away; to walk. Yeb. 84ᵃ כשהָלַכְתִּי וכ׳ when I left home to study with &c. Macc. 10ᵇ, a. e. בדרך שאדם רוצה לֵילֵךְ בה מוֹלִיכִין אותו whatever way one desires to go, one is led; a. v. fr.—הוֹלְכֵי מדברות travellers through the desert. Taan. …

- `2.2` Pi. to cause opening of the bowels. Y. Kil. I, 27ᵃ top מְהַלֶּכֶת את בני מיעים; cmp. הִילּוּךְ. [Y. Keth. XII, 35ᵇ top מהלך לפניהן אל הארץ, v. חָלַד.]
- `3.1` Hif. to lead; to carry. Macc. 10ᵇ, v. supra. Gitt. 4ᵃ המוֹלִיךְ וכ׳ he who carries abroad a letter of divorce, contrad. to המביא he who brings a letter from abroad. Ab. Zar. III, 9 יוֹלִיךְ הנאה וכ׳ let him cast the profit (one loaf’s value) into the Sea. Gitt. VI,…
- `4.1` Hithpa. to go away, withdraw. Cant. R. to V, 1; Gen. R. s. 19 (ref. to Gen. III, 8) מְהַלֵּךְ … אלא מִתְהַלֵּךְ וכ׳ it does not say m’hallekh (walking) but mith-hallekh, He hastened and went upward. Ib. מתהלך לו (ed. מת הלך, corr. acc., v. Matt. K.) is he (Adam) going…

- `1` (b. h.) [to be thin, shrunk, hard,] to be old. Gen. R. s. 48 (ref. to Gen. XVIII, 13) ואני זָקַנְתִּי מלעשות וכ׳ am I (the Lord) too old to do wonders?
- `2.1` Hif. to grow old. Snh. 100ᵇ הִזְקִינָה שמא וכ׳ when she arrives at old age, he is afraid lest &c. Erub. 56ᵃ מַזְקִינִים בחצי וכ׳ they age in the middle of their days (prematurely). Sabb. 152ᵃ כל זמן שמַזְקִינִין the older they grow; a. fr.
- `2.2` Hif. to make old, consider old (feeble). Gen. R. s. 48 אתם … ומזקינים חביריכם you consider each himself young, and each his partner old (Yalk. ib. 82 ומז׳ אדניכם and believe your Lord too old [to do wonders]); v. supra.
- `4.1` Nithpa. to become old, weak, frail. Y. Ber. IX, end 14ᶜ (ref. to prov. XXIII, 22) אם נִזְדַּקְּנָה אומתך וכ׳ if thy nation is decaying (in faith), stand up and fence her in (prevent her being trodden upon); Yalk. Prov. 960.—2) (cmp. דקדק) to be maturely considered, be…
- `4.2` Nithpa. (cmp. קשיש) to be hard, difficult. Tosef. Snh. VII, 7 (the presiding judge declares) (נִיזְקַן) נִזְקַן הדין ed. Zuck. (Var. נזדקן, נזדקק); discussed in Snh. 42ᵃ מאי נזד׳ הדין what does nizdakken mean? Does it mean קש דינא the case is hard (difficult, so as to…

- `1` preced.)
- `2` old man. Gen. R. s. 39, opp. בחור. Y. Bicc. III, 65ᶜ bot. עמידת ז׳ (Yalk. Lev. 670 מצות ז׳) the duty of standing up before an old man. Ḥag. 14ᵃ; a. fr.
- `3` elder, judge, scholar. Ib. (ref. to Is. III, 2) ז׳ זה שראוי וכ׳ zaken means one fit to sit in college sessions. Ber. 8ᵇ ז׳ ששכח וכ׳ a scholar who forgot what he had learned, &c., v. אוֹנֶס. Kidd. 32ᵇ אין ז׳ אלא חכם under zaken (Lev. XIX, 32) a scholar is meant…
- `4` grandfather, ancestor. Ex. R. s. 1 מעשה זְקֵנָן the conduct of their ancestor (Abraham). Pesik. Zakh., p. 27ᵇ; a. fr.—Fem. זְקֵנָה, זְקֵינָה. 1) old woman. Gen. R. s. 39.—Nidd. 9ᵃ ז׳ one who is past the change of life. Ib. 9ᵇ; a. fr.—2) grandmother, ancestress…

### `jastrow:חֲנַנְיָא` חֲנַנְיָא (pr. n. m.): Hanania;
- `1` Hanania;
- `2` H., one of the Babylonian exiles at the Babylonian court. Sabb. 67ᵃ אישתא דח׳ וכ׳ the fire prepared for H., Mishael and Azariah. Snh. 93ᵃ כי היכי … לחּ׳ וכ׳ as I tested H. &c.; a. v. fr.
- `3` several Tannaim and Amoraim (interchanging with חֲנִינָא q. v.); esp. Ḥ. b. ʿAkashia: Macc. III, 16. Tosef. Shek. III, 18.—H. b. T’radion, a martyr of the Adrianic persecution. Tosef. Kel. B. Kam. IV, 17. Ab. Zar. 17ᵇ, sq. (חנינא). Taan. 16ᵇ; a. fr.—H. b. ʿAka…

### `jastrow:חֵן` חֵן (m.): grace, favor; loveliness. Ab. Zar. 20ᵃ (ref. to תחנם, Deut. VII, 2) לא תתן להם חן ascribe no gracefulness to them (pay no attention to their beauty). Yeb. 63ᵇ (quot. fr. Ben. Sira) אשת חן coquette. Keth. 17ᵃ; Snh. 14ᵃ, v. יַעֲלָה. Succ. 49ᵇ אדם שיש עליו חן (Ms…
- `1` grace, favor; loveliness. Ab. Zar. 20ᵃ (ref. to תחנם, Deut. VII, 2) לא תתן להם חן ascribe no gracefulness to them (pay no attention to their beauty). Yeb. 63ᵇ (quot. fr. Ben. Sira) אשת חן coquette. Keth. 17ᵃ; Snh. 14ᵃ, v. יַעֲלָה. Succ. 49ᵇ אדם שיש עליו חן (Ms…

### `jastrow:חֵפֶץ` חֵפֶץ (m.): preced.)
- `1` preced.)
- `2` thing (held in hand), object. B. Mets. IV, 10 בכמה ח׳ זה how much is this worth? Cant. R. to I, 4 כל ח׳ טוב any good thing; a. fr.
- `3` concern, business; desire, desirable object. Ib.; Pesik. Sos, p. 147ᵃ אין לי ח׳ טוב ממך I have nothing more desirable than thyself. Koh. R. to V, 7 נעשה חֶפְצוֹ his desire was fulfilled. Num. R. s. 19 אל תחזירני מן חֶפְצִי וכ׳ do not turn me off from (refuse) …

### `jastrow:חַגַּי` חַגַּי: (b. h.) pr. n. m.
- `1` (b. h.) pr. n. m.
- `2` Haggai, the Prophet. Naz. 53ᵃ. Yeb. 16ᵃ; a. fr.
- `3` also חַגַּיי, name of several Amoraim. Y. Ber. II, 5ᵇ top.—Y. Dem. III, 23ᵇ bot. B. Kam. 42ᵃ, v. חַגָּא II. V. Fr. M’bo, p. 79ᵇ, sq.

### `jastrow:חַיִּים` חַיִּים (m. pl.): life, support; health. Sabb. 33ᵇ חַיֵּי עולם everlasting life (future world), ח׳ שעה temporary life (physical wants).—חיי נפש necessaries of life. Y. Ned. IX, 42ᶜ the support of the poor of one’s own place. Pes. 113ᵇ חַיֵּיהֶם אינם חיים whose life is no life (…

### `jastrow:חַמָּה` חַמָּה (f.): sun. Ber. 59ᵇ ח׳ בתקופתה the sun starting on his new cycle (of twenty eight years). Num. R. s. 14 י"ב הדשים לח׳ the twelve solar months. Ned. III, 7 רואי הח׳ those seeing (or feeling) the sun; שלא נתכוין … שהח׳ וכ׳ for he meant him whom the sun sees; a. v. fr.…
- `1` sun. Ber. 59ᵇ ח׳ בתקופתה the sun starting on his new cycle (of twenty eight years). Num. R. s. 14 י"ב הדשים לח׳ the twelve solar months. Ned. III, 7 רואי הח׳ those seeing (or feeling) the sun; שלא נתכוין … שהח׳ וכ׳ for he meant him whom the sun sees; a. v. fr.…
- `2` fever. Sabb. 137ᵃ, a. e., v. חָלַץ. Y. Sabb. XIX, end, 17ᵇ, v. אָחַז. Gen. R. s. 19, a. e., v. אַבְאָבִית. Y. Sabb. I, end, 4ᵇ (prov.) פת חמה חַמָּתָהּ בצדה hot bread has its heat by its side, i.e. eating hot bread causes fever; a. fr.
- `3` radish. Ab. Zar. 28ᵇ bot., v. חַמָּא.—V. חַמַּת.

### `jastrow:חָזַק` חָזַק: [to squeeze together,] (neut. verb) to be thick, solid; to be strong. Y. Ber. I, 2ᶜ bot. (ref. to Gen. I, 6) יֶחֱזַק הרקיע וכ׳ let the expanse become solidified, let it coagulate, congeal; Gen. R. s. 4; (Yalk. ib. 5 יתחזק, Hithpa.).
- `1` [to squeeze together,] (neut. verb) to be thick, solid; to be strong. Y. Ber. I, 2ᶜ bot. (ref. to Gen. I, 6) יֶחֱזַק הרקיע וכ׳ let the expanse become solidified, let it coagulate, congeal; Gen. R. s. 4; (Yalk. ib. 5 יתחזק, Hithpa.).
- `2.1` Pi. to join, repair, tighten, strengthen. Snh. 94ᵃ (expl. חזקיה) שחי׳ את ישראל וכ׳ (Ms. M. שהחזיק, Hif.) he joined Israel to their Father &c. Ib. שחִזְּקוֹ יה the Lord strengthened him. Pes. 45ᵃ שעשוי לחַזֵּק Ms. M. where the dough in the cracks is put in for repa…
- `3.1` Hif. same, v. supra.— same, v. supra.—ה׳ טובה ל־ to attach merit to, to account as merit, to be grateful. Ab. II, 8 אל תַּחֲזִיק ט׳ לעצמך do not claim credit for it (be not proud). Yoma 86ᵇ אלא שמַחֲזִיק לו ט׳ but He even gives him credit (for his sins when he repe…
- `3.2` Hif. (with יד) to strengthen, to encourage, abet. Gitt. V, 9 לפי שאין מַחֲזִיקִין וכ׳ because we must not encourage (by favors) those who do wrong. Ib. ומחזיקין ידי עכו"ם וכ׳ we may encourage (greet with תחזקנה ידיכם, Zech. VIII, 9) gentiles at agricultural work in…
- `3.3` Hif. to hold, contain. Ib. 57ᵃ אין עורו מחזיק וכ׳ its skin (once flayed) can not again cover its entire body (it shrinks). Ib. (in Chald. dict.) אפי׳ שתין … לא מ׳ it would not have room even for sixty myriads of reeds. Par. VII, 8 בשביל שתַּחֲזִיק וכ׳ in order that…
- `3.4` Hif. (with ב) to take a hold of, seize, take possession. Ḥull. 4ᵃ, a. e. כל מצוה שהֶחֱזִיקוּ וכ׳ whatever Jewish law the Samaritans have adopted &c., v. דִּקְדֵּק. B. Mets. I, 4 וה׳ בה and took a hold of it. B. Bath. III, 3 במַחֲזִיק when one is in possession (basi…
- `3.5` Hif. (v. חֲזָקָה) to presume, to be under a certain impression, to be convinced. Y. Kidd. IV, 66ᵇ היו מַחֲזִיקִין בו שהוא בנו וכ׳ if people were under the impression that a certain person was their neighbor’s son, but in his dying hour he declared &c. Ib. היו מ׳ או…
- `4.1` Hof. (denom. of חֲזָקָה) to be presumed, be held for, be known for. Gitt. 14ᵃ בשה׳ כפרן when the man is known to be a liar. Shebu. 34ᵇ, a. fr. ה׳ כפרן (in such a case) he is considered a confirmed liar.—Y. Kidd. l. c. bot. הוּחְזְקוּ if they were generally assumed …
- `4.2` Hof. held in possession, adhered to. Bekh. VIII, 9 ולא בראוי כבמ׳ nor does he take a double share of what is coming due to the estate as he does of what is held in possession; B. Bath. 55ᵃ; a. fr.—Sabb. 130ᵃ עדיין היא מוּחֲזֶקֶת בידם it is still strongly adhered to…
- `4.3` Hof. being known, approved. Sifra K’dosh. Par. 3, ch. V במ׳ לך when he is known to thee (to be a proselyte); a. e.
- `4.4` Hof. being sure, convinced, knowing from experience. Keth. 25ᵇ מוּחֲזַקְנִי בזה וכ׳ (= מ׳ אני) I know this man to be a priest; a. e.—Sifré Num. 1, v. supra.
- `6.1` Nithpa. to become solid, strong. Yalk. Gen. 5 יִתְחַזֵּק, v. preced.
- `6.2` Nithpa. to feel encouraged, take courage. Ber. 32ᵃ נ׳ בתפלה became emboldened to pray.

### `jastrow:חָזַר` חָזַר: to go around (searching). Koh. R. to VII, 8 הוא חוֹזֵר עליה he searches it again (tries to recover his scholarship); ib. יכול הוא לַחֲזוֹר עליה he may recover it; a. e.; v. infra.
- `1` to go around (searching). Koh. R. to VII, 8 הוא חוֹזֵר עליה he searches it again (tries to recover his scholarship); ib. יכול הוא לַחֲזוֹר עליה he may recover it; a. e.; v. infra.
- `2` to turn around, return; to retract, repent. Ib. חֲזוֹר בך come back (repent)! Eduy. V, 6 חזור בך withdraw thy opposition.—Dem. IV, 1 חָזְרָה למקומה came back to its place (was mixed up again). Maas. Sh. I, 5 יֶחְזְרוּ דמים וכ׳ the money shall return to its for…
- `3.1` Pi. to go around from one to the other. Yeb. 53ᵃ צריכה לְחַזֵּר על וכ׳ she must apply to all the brothers successively; (ib. 26ᵇ; 51ᵇ לַחֲזוֹר). Y. Taan. IV, 68ᵃ bot. חִיזַּרְנוּ על כל וכ׳ we searched the whole Bible; Y. Ab. Zar. I, beg. 39ᵃ חי׳ בכל וכ׳; (Sifré De…
- `4.1` Hif. to restore, give back. Ber. 27ᵇ המַחֲזִיר שלים לרבו he who returns the plain salutation (shalom) of his teacher (without adding, ‘My teacher’). Ib. 32ᵇ לא ה׳ לו שלום did not answer his greeting. B. Mets. 7ᵇ יַחֲזִיר לאשה he must restore (the lost document) to …
- `4.2` Hif. to revoke; to reconsider, to grant a new trial. Gitt. VI, 3 אם רצה להַחֲזִיר לא יַחֲזִיר if the husband wants to revoke the letter of divorce, he cannot do so.—Snh. IV, 1 דיני נפשות מַחֲזִירִין וכ׳ in capital cases verdicts may be reconsidered in favor of the …
- `4.3` Hif. to make one read over, to cause correction. Y. Meg. IV, 74ᵈ מחזירין אותו we order him to read it over again correctly. Ber. 29ᵃ; a. fr.

### `jastrow:חָכְמָה` חָכְמָה (f.): wisdom, learning, art. Ber. 17ᵃ תכלית ח׳ וכ׳ the perfection of wisdom is repentance and good deeds. Ib. 33ᵃ ברכת ח׳ the benediction of wisdom (the fourth of the Prayer of Benedictions). Gen. R. s. 17 חָכְמָתוֹ מרובה וכ׳ his (Adam’s) wisdom is greater than your…
- `1` wisdom, learning, art. Ber. 17ᵃ תכלית ח׳ וכ׳ the perfection of wisdom is repentance and good deeds. Ib. 33ᵃ ברכת ח׳ the benediction of wisdom (the fourth of the Prayer of Benedictions). Gen. R. s. 17 חָכְמָתוֹ מרובה וכ׳ his (Adam’s) wisdom is greater than your…

### `jastrow:חָכָם` חָכָם (m.): v. Fl. to Levy Talm. Dict. II, 2042) [retentive,]
- `1` v. Fl. to Levy Talm. Dict. II, 2042) [retentive,]
- `2` one who knows. חֲכַם הרזים He who knows the secrets (minds of men). Ber. 58ᵃ; Tosef. ib. VII (VI), 2; Num. R. s. 21, beg.
- `3` wise man, scholar; esp. Ḥakham, a scholar’s title, less than Rabbi. Ab. IV, 1 איזהו ח׳ who is a wise man? Snh. 21ᵃ (ref. to II Sam. XIII, 3) איש ח׳ לרשעה a man wise for wickedness (artful). Gitt. 67ᵃ ח׳ וסופר is a scholar and a scribe; ח׳ לכשירצה might be a sc…

### `jastrow:חָמֵשׁ` חָמֵשׁ (m.): (b. h.) five (numeral letter ה׳). Snh. V, 3 בחמש (sub. שעות) during the fifth hour of the day. Ib. בחמשה (sub. ימים) on the fifth of the month.—Sabb. 77ᵇ חמשה אימות וכ׳ (sub. מִינֵי), v. אֵימָה. B. Mets. IV, 9 תשלומי ארבעה וח׳ fines of the fourfold or fivefold…
- `1` (b. h.) five (numeral letter ה׳). Snh. V, 3 בחמש (sub. שעות) during the fifth hour of the day. Ib. בחמשה (sub. ימים) on the fifth of the month.—Sabb. 77ᵇ חמשה אימות וכ׳ (sub. מִינֵי), v. אֵימָה. B. Mets. IV, 9 תשלומי ארבעה וח׳ fines of the fourfold or fivefold…

### `jastrow:טוֹב` טוֹב: Pa. טַיֵּיב, Af. אָטֵיב to do good, to favor. Targ. Y. I Ex. XXIII, 2 למְטַיְּיבָא; Y. II למֵיטְבָא. V. יְטַב.
- `1` Pa. טַיֵּיב, Af. אָטֵיב to do good, to favor. Targ. Y. I Ex. XXIII, 2 למְטַיְּיבָא; Y. II למֵיטְבָא. V. יְטַב.

### `jastrow:טוֹב I` טוֹב I: (b. h.) to be good, fit, handsome, valuable.
- `1` (b. h.) to be good, fit, handsome, valuable.
- `2.1` Hif. to prepare, outfit, dress, adorn. Gen. R. s. 26, v. טַבָּת. Gen. R. s. 83 (play on מהיטבאל, Gen. XXXVI, 39) מְטִיבֵי אלוהות היו they were dressers of idols; שהיו מֵטִיבִין עצמן וכ׳ they adorned themselves in honor of the idols. Ib. מטיבי נשים וכ׳ they dressed …
- `2.2` Hif. to turn a dream unto good (saying, ‘I have dreamt a good dream’). Ber. 55ᵇ יְטִיבֶנּוּ בפני וכ׳ he shall turn it in the presence of three persons, and say &c.
- `2.3` Hif. to do good, be beneficent. Ib. IX, 2 הטוב והמטיב who is good and beneficent. Taan. 31ᵃ תקנו … הטוב והמ׳ they introduced in Jabneh the benediction ‘who is good &c.’ (in the grace after meal).—Kidd. I, 10 מטיבין לו good will be done to him; a. fr.—Part. Hof. מוּ…
- `3.1` Pi. to improve a field, to till oftener than usual. Y. Shebi. IV, 35ᵇ top טִיְּיבָהּ וכ׳ if, after he improved a field, he died &c.
- `4.1` Nithpa. to be improved. Shebi. IV, 2; Tosef. ib. III, 10; a. e.—Denom. טִיּיוּב.

### `jastrow:טוֹב II` טוֹב II (f.): (b. h.)
- `1` (b. h.)
- `2` good; a good thing. Ber. 5ᵃ (ref. to Ps. XXXIX, 3) אין ט׳ אלא וכ׳ under good the Torah is meant. Ib. 60ᵇ זו מדה ט׳ this refers to a good dispensation; a. v. fr.—יום טוב (abbr. י"ט) festival. Bets. I, 1; a. fr.—כי טוב (ref. to Gen. I, 4) day-time. Pes. 2ᵃ.—Ber.…
- `3` noble, elder.—Pl. טוֹבִים. Tosef. Shek. II, 16 בני ט׳ of noble descent; a. fr.—טוֹבֵי העיר representatives of the town. Meg. 27ᵃ

### `jastrow:יְהוּדָה` יְהוּדָה: (b. h.) pr. n. Judah,
- `1` (b. h.) pr. n. Judah,
- `2` son of Jacob; tribe of Judah. Pes. 50ᵃ. Yoma 12ᵃ, a. e. מחלקו של י׳ from the area of Judah.—Gen. R. s. 85; a. v. fr.
- `3` name of several Tannaim; a) R. J. b. B’thera in Babylonia Ber. 22ᵃ; a. fr.—b) J. b. Tabbai, chief of the Sanhedrin in the days of queen Salome. Ab. I, 8. Ḥag. II, 2; a. fr.—c) R. J. the priest. Eduy. VIII, 3.—d) R. J. b. Baba. Ib. 2. Sabb. 62ᵇ; a. fr.—e) R. J.…
- `4` name of several Amoraim, the most renowned of whom is R. J. (b. Ezekiel), a Babylonian. Keth. 110ᵇ bot.—Y. Taan. I, 64ᵇ top; a. fr.—V. Fr. M’bo p. 91ᵃ
- `5` Judaea, the southern province of Palestine. Keth. I, 5 (12ᵃ); Tosef. ib. I, 4. Kidd. 6ᵃ; a. v. fr.

### `jastrow:יְרוּשָׁלַיִם` יְרוּשָׁלַיִם: (b. h.) pr. n. pl. Jerusalem. Succ. 51ᵇ מי … י׳ בתפארתה וכ׳ he who has not seen J. in her glory, has never seen &c. Gen. R. s. 43 (interpret. מלכי־צדק, Gen. XIV, 18) צדק נקראת י׳ Jerusalem is called by the name of Tsedek (Righteousness; ref. to Is. I, 26). Num…
- `1` (b. h.) pr. n. pl. Jerusalem. Succ. 51ᵇ מי … י׳ בתפארתה וכ׳ he who has not seen J. in her glory, has never seen &c. Gen. R. s. 43 (interpret. מלכי־צדק, Gen. XIV, 18) צדק נקראת י׳ Jerusalem is called by the name of Tsedek (Righteousness; ref. to Is. I, 26). Num…

### `jastrow:יִשְׂרָאֵל` יִשְׂרָאֵל (pr. n.): Israel.
- `1` Israel.
- `2` I. (Jacob), the patriarch. Gen. R. s. 68 י׳ סבא the patriarch I. the patriarch I. (not the people); a. fr.
- `3` I., the people. Ber. 6ᵃ; a. fr.—שונאי י׳ the enemies of Israel, often euphem. for Israel. Ib. 32ᵃ נתמוטטו … של ש׳ י׳ the feet of Israel would totter (in judgment); a. fr.—V. כְּנֶסֶת.
- `4` י׳ (= בן י׳) an Israelite, a) one not belonging to the priestly or levitic tribe; b) a Jew, opp. נכרי, גוי. Gitt. V, 8. Tosef. Kidd. V, 3; a. fr.—Tosef. Ab. Zar. III, 5, sq. Ab. Zar. II, 6; a. fr.—בַּת י׳ an Israelitish woman. Tosef. l. c. 1; 3; a. fr.—Pl. יִש…

### `jastrow:יָד` יָד: f. (rarely m.) (b. h.;
- `1` f. (rarely m.) (b. h.;
- `2` hand; forefoot; handle. Ex. R. s. 42, end מיד ליד from hand to hand, directly, opp. ע"י שלוחו through his messenger.—י׳ רחבה a wide hand, liberality. Y. Ḥag. I, 76ᶜ top עני ויָדוֹ ר׳ a poor man who is liberal, opp. י׳ מעוטה stingy.—Ḥull. 58ᵇ, a. fr. בְּיָד on …
- `3` power, authority, possession, share. B. Mets. 70ᵇ, v. אֶמְצַע. Kidd. 3ᵇ קטנה דלית לה יד וכ׳ a minor who cannot accept a betrothal for herself. Ned. 88ᵇ יד אשה כיד בעלה the wife’s possession is her husband’s possession. Kidd. 23ᵃ גיטו ויָדוֹ באים כאחד his lette…
- `4` portion, part.—Pl. יָדוֹת. Tosef. Men. IX, 10 שתי י׳ two thirds; a. fr.—מִיַּד (= מיד ליד) at once, directly, immediately. Tosef. Dem. VIII, 7, v. חָלַל Hif.—Y. Pes. VI, 33ᵃ מיד כל מי וכ׳ presently, every one whose Passover offering was a lamb &c.; a. v. fr.— …

### `jastrow:יָסַף` יָסַף: , Hif. הוֹסִיף to heap up, to add (with על). Snh. XI, 3 להוֹסִיף על דברי וכ׳ thus adding to the words of the Scribes (against Deut. IV, 2). Ib. 88ᵇ ויש בו להו׳ when there is a possibility to add. Ib. ואם ה׳ גורע and if he did add, he diminishes (violates the l…

- `2.1` Nithpa. , Hithpa. הִתּוֹסַף to be added; to be added to, increase, wax. Mekh. Bo. s. 16; Yalk. Ex. 217 והשני נ׳ לו and the second name was added to the first (without abrogating the first). Ex. R. s. 7, beg. נ׳ לו עוד וכ׳ two additional years (of imprisonment) were gi…

### `jastrow:יָשַׁב` יָשַׁב: (b. h.)
- `1` (b. h.)
- `2` to sit down, rest; to dwell, remain; to be inactive. Macc. III, 15 כל חיוֹשֵׁב ולא עבר וכ׳ whoever is inactive and commits no sin, i.e. omits to do wrong; Kidd. 39ᵇ.—Erub. 100ᵃ שֵׁב ואל תעשה עדיף ‘sit and do nothing’, i.e. not to act in doubtful cases, is bett…
- `3` to be settled (v. יִישּׁוּב), be inhabited. Num. R. s. 4 הגבעה … יוֹשְׁבִים Gibeah and Kiryath Jearim were settled at the same time.—Erub. 86ᵃ (ref. to Ps. LXI, 8) אימתי יושב עולם לפני וכ׳ (Rashi a. Ar. יֵשֵׁב) when is the world settled (evenly balanced) befor…
- `4.1` Pi. to settle, put in place, to arrange evenly. Y. Sabb. XII, beg. 13ᶜ הבנאי י׳ את האבן וכ׳ the builder that placed a stone on top of the row (v. דִּימוֹס I). Ib.ᵈ top; Y. Pes. VIII, 33ᵇ top מפני שהוא כמְיַישֵּׁב בידו because it is like settling the web with one’s…
- `4.2` Pi. to settle, cultivate, populate. Ber. 58ᵇ … עתיד להחזירו וליַישְּׁבוֹ Ms. M. (ed. ליִישּׁוּבוֹ) the Lord will again people it. Sot. 22ᵃ מְיַישְּׁבֵי עולם cultivators of the world.—Part. pass. מְיוּשָּׁב, f. מְיוּשֶּׁבֶת a) seated. Meg. 21ᵃ. Y. Gitt. VII, 48ᵈ to…
- `5.1` Hif. to seat, place, appoint; to settle. Yoma 38ᵃ sq.; Tosef. ib. II, 7 במקומך יוֹשִׁיבוּךָ people will finally seat thee in the place which thou deservest. Tosef. Sabb. VI (VII), 17 (among superstitious practices) חמוֹשֶׁבֶת אפרוחים (ו)אמרה איני מוֹשַׁבְתָּן וכ׳ a…
- `6.1` Nif. to be inhabited. Pirké d’R. El. ch. XLIII ארץ נוֹשֶׁבֶת an inhabited land, settlement; (Koh. R. to I, 15 יִישּׁוּכ).
- `7.1` Hithpa. , Nithpa. נִתְיַישֵּׁב
- `7.2` Hithpa. to be settled, colonized. Ber. 31ᵃ כל ארץ שגזר … ליישוב נִתְיַישְּׁבָה every land which Adam designated for settlement, has been settled.
- `7.3` Hithpa. to be at ease, to be refreshed, to come to. Yoma 82ᵃ אם נִתְיַשְּׁבָה דעתה if she feels that her craving has been gratified. Lam. R. to I, 11 (ref. to להשיב נפש, ib.) עד כמה מִתְיַישֶּׁבֶת נפש how much is required for one fainting from hunger (v. בּוֹלִימוֹס) …

- `8.4` Hif. to skip over; to forego, postpone.—ה׳ על מדותיו, v. מִדָּה.—Yoma 33ᵃ; 58ᵇ, a. fr. אין מעבירין על המצות you must not forego the occasion for performing a religious act. Erub. 64ᵇ אין מעבירין על האוכלין you must not pass by eatables (and let them lie in the stre…

- `2` magistracy, office. Y. R. Hash. I, 57ᵇ bot. והעכירו מרֵאשִׁיתוֹ he removed him (the chief magistrate of Gadara) from his office; (Bab. ib. 22ᵃ מגדולתו).
